using System;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Mail;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;

namespace QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.Services
{
    public static class AuthService
    {
        // 1. NHẬP GMAIL CỦA BẠN VÀO ĐÂY:
        private static readonly string SenderEmail = "kaytjungle@gmail.com";

        // 2. NHẬP MÃ 16 KÝ TỰ VÀO ĐÂY (VÍ DỤ: "abcd efgh ijkl mnop"):
        private static readonly string AppPassword = "ojwy azud ahht ukzb";

        public static string HashPassword(string password)
        {
            using (SHA256 sha256 = SHA256.Create())
            {
                byte[] bytes = sha256.ComputeHash(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(password));
                StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
                foreach (byte b in bytes)
                {
                    builder.Append(b.ToString("x2"));
                }
                return builder.ToString();
            }
        }

        public static string GenerateOTP()
        {
            Random rnd = new Random();
            return rnd.Next(100000, 999999).ToString();
        }

        public static bool SendOtpEmail(string toEmail, string otpCode, string actionTitle, out string errorMsg)
        {
            errorMsg = "";
            try
            {
                // Đoạn chặn Demo đã bị xóa hoàn toàn ở đây!
                // Mã bây giờ sẽ gửi thẳng tới máy chủ Google.

                string cleanAppPassword = AppPassword.Replace(" ", "").Trim();

                using (MailMessage mail = new MailMessage())
                {
                    mail.From = new MailAddress(SenderEmail, "COFFEE FEFA - NHÓM 8");
                    mail.To.Add(toEmail.Trim());
                    mail.Subject = $"[COFFEE FEFA] Mã OTP {actionTitle}";
                    mail.IsBodyHtml = true;
                    mail.Body = $@"
                        <div style='font-family: Segoe UI, sans-serif; padding: 20px; border: 2px solid #6F4E37; border-radius: 10px; max-width: 520px;'>
                            <h2 style='color: #3E2723; margin-top: 0;'>☕ HỆ THỐNG QUẢN LÝ CAFE - NHÓM 8</h2>
                            <p>Xin chào,</p>
                            <p>Hệ thống đang thực hiện yêu cầu: <b>{actionTitle}</b> cho địa chỉ Gmail này.</p>
                            <p>Mã xác thực OTP gồm 6 chữ số của bạn là:</p>
                            <div style='font-size: 28px; font-weight: bold; color: #D35400; letter-spacing: 6px; background: #FDF2E9; padding: 14px; text-align: center; border-radius: 6px;'>
                                {otpCode}
                            </div>
                            <p style='color: #555; font-size: 13px; margin-top: 15px;'>
                                * Mã này có hiệu lực trong vòng <b>5 phút</b>. Tuyệt đối không chia sẻ mã này cho người khác.
                            </p>
                        </div>";

                    using (SmtpClient smtp = new SmtpClient("smtp.gmail.com", 587))
                    {
                        smtp.EnableSsl = true;
                        smtp.UseDefaultCredentials = false;
                        smtp.Credentials = new NetworkCredential(SenderEmail, cleanAppPassword);
                        smtp.Send(mail);
                    }
                }
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Bắt đúng lỗi thực tế từ Google nếu mật khẩu ứng dụng bị sai
                errorMsg = ex.Message;
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
}


/* =========================================
   FILE: NetworkClient.cs 
========================================= */

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Configuration;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.IO;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using Protocol;

namespace QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.Services
{
    public sealed class NetworkClient
    {
        private static readonly Lazy<NetworkClient> _instance = new Lazy<NetworkClient>(() => new NetworkClient());
        public static NetworkClient Instance => _instance.Value;

        private TcpClient _tcpClient;
        private NetworkStream _stream;
        private readonly SemaphoreSlim _writeLock = new SemaphoreSlim(1, 1);
        private readonly SemaphoreSlim _connectLock = new SemaphoreSlim(1, 1);
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, TaskCompletionSource<PacketMessage>> _pendingRequests =
            new ConcurrentDictionary<string, TaskCompletionSource<PacketMessage>>();

        private CancellationTokenSource _cts;
        private bool _isStarted = false;

        // Đọc IP Azure VM từ App.config (để trống nếu muốn tự dò UDP trong LAN)
        public string ConfiguredServerIp { get; set; } =
            (ConfigurationManager.AppSettings["ServerIp"] ?? "").Trim();

        public string ServerHost { get; private set; } = "127.0.0.1";
        public int ServerPort { get; private set; } = FramingHelper.DefaultTcpPort;
        public bool IsConnected => _tcpClient != null && _tcpClient.Connected && _stream != null;

        public event Action<MsgType, string> OnServerEventReceived;
        public event Action<bool, string> OnConnectionStateChanged;

        public readonly List<string> ChatHistory = new List<string>();
        private Form _activeChatForm = null;

        private NetworkClient() { }

        public void Start()
        {
            if (_isStarted) return;
            _isStarted = true;
            _cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            Task.Run(() => MaintainConnectionLoopAsync(_cts.Token));
            Task.Run(() => HeartbeatLoopAsync(_cts.Token));
        }

        // Tự dò Server qua UDP Broadcast cổng 9001 (chỉ dùng khi chạy LAN & không cấu hình IP Azure)
        private async Task<IPEndPoint> DiscoverLanServerAsync(int timeoutMs = 1200)
        {
            return await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                try
                {
                    using (UdpClient udp = new UdpClient())
                    {
                        udp.EnableBroadcast = true;
                        udp.Client.ReceiveTimeout = timeoutMs;

                        byte[] reqBytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(FramingHelper.DiscoveryRequest);
                        udp.Send(reqBytes, reqBytes.Length, new IPEndPoint(IPAddress.Broadcast, FramingHelper.DefaultUdpPort));

                        IPEndPoint remoteEp = new IPEndPoint(IPAddress.Any, 0);
                        byte[] respBytes = udp.Receive(ref remoteEp);
                        string resp = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(respBytes).Trim();

                        if (resp.StartsWith(FramingHelper.DiscoveryResponsePrefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            string portStr = resp.Substring(FramingHelper.DiscoveryResponsePrefix.Length);
                            int port = int.TryParse(portStr, out int p) ? p : FramingHelper.DefaultTcpPort;
                            return new IPEndPoint(remoteEp.Address, port);
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch { }
                return new IPEndPoint(IPAddress.Loopback, FramingHelper.DefaultTcpPort);
            }).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }

        private async Task<bool> EnsureConnectedAsync()
        {
            if (IsConnected) return true;

            await _connectLock.WaitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
            try
            {
                if (IsConnected) return true;

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ConfiguredServerIp))
                {
                    ServerHost = ConfiguredServerIp;
                    ServerPort = int.TryParse(ConfigurationManager.AppSettings["ServerPort"], out int cfgPort)
                        ? cfgPort
                        : FramingHelper.DefaultTcpPort;
                }
                else
                {
                    IPEndPoint ep = await DiscoverLanServerAsync(1000).ConfigureAwait(false);
                    ServerHost = ep.Address.ToString();
                    ServerPort = ep.Port;
                }

                _tcpClient?.Close();
                _tcpClient = new TcpClient();

                var connectTask = _tcpClient.ConnectAsync(ServerHost, ServerPort);
                var finished = await Task.WhenAny(connectTask, Task.Delay(4000)).ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (finished != connectTask || !_tcpClient.Connected)
                {
                    _tcpClient.Close();
                    return false;
                }

                _stream = _tcpClient.GetStream();
                OnConnectionStateChanged?.Invoke(true, $"Đã kết nối TCP ({ServerHost}:{ServerPort})");

                _ = Task.Run(() => ReceiveLoopAsync(_cts?.Token ?? CancellationToken.None));
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                OnConnectionStateChanged?.Invoke(false, $"Lỗi kết nối ({ServerHost}:{ServerPort}): {ex.Message}");
                return false;
            }
            finally
            {
                _connectLock.Release();
            }
        }

        private async Task MaintainConnectionLoopAsync(CancellationToken token)
        {
            while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)
            {
                if (!IsConnected)
                {
                    await EnsureConnectedAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
                }
                await Task.Delay(3000, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }
        }

        private async Task HeartbeatLoopAsync(CancellationToken token)
        {
            while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)
            {
                await Task.Delay(10000, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (IsConnected)
                {
                    try { await SendPacketInternalAsync(new PacketMessage { Type = MsgType.PING }).ConfigureAwait(false); }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
        }

        private async Task SendPacketInternalAsync(PacketMessage packet)
        {
            await _writeLock.WaitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
            try
            {
                if (!IsConnected) throw new IOException("Chưa kết nối tới CafeServer.");
                await FramingHelper.SendPacketAsync(_stream, packet).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }
            finally
            {
                _writeLock.Release();
            }
        }

        private async Task ReceiveLoopAsync(CancellationToken token)
        {
            try
            {
                while (!token.IsCancellationRequested && IsConnected)
                {
                    PacketMessage packet = await FramingHelper.ReceivePacketAsync(_stream).ConfigureAwait(false);
                    if (packet == null) break;

                    if (packet.Type == MsgType.PONG) continue;

                    // Nếu là phản hồi của 1 Request đang chờ -> trả về đúng TaskCompletionSource
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(packet.RequestId) &&
                        _pendingRequests.TryRemove(packet.RequestId, out var tcs))
                    {
                        tcs.TrySetResult(packet);
                        continue;
                    }

                    // Nếu là gói Server Broadcast (Realtime Push)
                    if (packet.Type == MsgType.CHAT_MESSAGE)
                    {
                        lock (ChatHistory)
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                var obj = JObject.Parse(packet.PayloadJson);
                                ChatHistory.Add(obj["Text"]?.ToString() ?? packet.PayloadJson);
                            }
                            catch { ChatHistory.Add(packet.PayloadJson); }
                        }
                    }

                    OnServerEventReceived?.Invoke(packet.Type, packet.PayloadJson);
                }
            }
            catch { }
            finally
            {
                foreach (var kv in _pendingRequests)
                {
                    if (_pendingRequests.TryRemove(kv.Key, out var tcs))
                        tcs.TrySetException(new IOException("Mất kết nối tới CafeServer."));
                }
                try { _tcpClient?.Close(); } catch { }
                _stream = null;
                OnConnectionStateChanged?.Invoke(false, "Mất kết nối TCP - Đang kết nối lại...");
            }
        }

        // Hàm gửi Request chuẩn Protocol và chờ phản hồi theo RequestId
        public async Task<PacketMessage> SendRequestAsync(MsgType type, object payload = null, int timeoutMs = 8000)
        {
            if (!await EnsureConnectedAsync().ConfigureAwait(false))
                throw new Exception($"Không kết nối được tới CafeServer ({ServerHost}:{ServerPort}).");

            var req = new PacketMessage
            {
                RequestId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"),
                Type = type,
                PayloadJson = payload == null ? "{}" : JsonConvert.SerializeObject(payload)
            };

            var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<PacketMessage>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
            _pendingRequests[req.RequestId] = tcs;

            try
            {
                await SendPacketInternalAsync(req).ConfigureAwait(false);
                var completed = await Task.WhenAny(tcs.Task, Task.Delay(timeoutMs)).ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (completed != tcs.Task)
                    throw new TimeoutException($"Server không phản hồi yêu cầu {type} sau {timeoutMs / 1000}s.");

                return await tcs.Task.ConfigureAwait(false);
            }
            finally
            {
                _pendingRequests.TryRemove(req.RequestId, out _);
            }
        }

        private static T DeserializeOrThrow<T>(PacketMessage res)
        {
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(res.Message) ? res.ErrorCode : res.Message);
            return JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<T>(res.PayloadJson);
        }

        // ==================== API AUTH (CHO FrmAuth) ====================

        public async Task<UserDTO> LoginAsync(string username, string password)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.LOGIN_REQ, new { Username = username, Password = password }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) return null;
            return JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<UserDTO>(res.PayloadJson);
        }

        // ==================== API POS & KDS (TƯƠNG THÍCH FrmMainPOS / FrmKDS) ====================

        public List<TableDTO> GetAllTables()
        {
            var res = SendRequestAsync(MsgType.GET_TABLES_REQ).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            return DeserializeOrThrow<List<TableDTO>>(res);
        }

        public List<ProductDTO> GetActiveProducts()
        {
            var res = SendRequestAsync(MsgType.GET_ACTIVE_PRODUCTS_REQ).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            return DeserializeOrThrow<List<ProductDTO>>(res);
        }

        public DataTable GetBillDetailsByTable(int tableId)
        {
            var res = SendRequestAsync(MsgType.GET_BILL_DETAILS_REQ, new { TableId = tableId }).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            var items = DeserializeOrThrow<List<OrderItemDTO>>(res);

            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            dt.Columns.Add("BillDetailId", typeof(int));
            dt.Columns.Add("Tên món", typeof(string));
            dt.Columns.Add("SL", typeof(int));
            dt.Columns.Add("Đơn giá", typeof(decimal));
            dt.Columns.Add("Thành tiền", typeof(decimal));
            dt.Columns.Add("Trạng thái", typeof(string));

            foreach (var it in items)
                dt.Rows.Add(it.BillDetailId, it.ProductName, it.Quantity, it.UnitPrice, it.SubTotal, it.KitchenStatus);
            return dt;
        }

        public bool VerifyManagerAccount(string username, string password, out string managerFullName)
        {
            var res = SendRequestAsync(MsgType.VERIFY_MANAGER_REQ, new { Username = username, Password = password }).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            var dto = DeserializeOrThrow<ManagerVerifyResponseDTO>(res);
            managerFullName = dto.ManagerFullName ?? "";
            return dto.IsApproved;
        }

        public async Task AddItemToTableAsync(int tableId, int productId, int quantity)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.ADD_ITEM_REQ, new { TableId = tableId, ProductId = productId, Quantity = quantity }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
        }

        public async Task<int> SendTableOrderToKitchenAsync(int tableId, string tableName)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.SEND_KITCHEN_REQ, new { TableId = tableId }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
            int count = JObject.Parse(res.PayloadJson)["SentCount"]?.Value<int>() ?? 0;
            if (count > 0)
                await SendChatAsync($"[Quầy Thu Ngân]: Đã gửi {count} món của {tableName} xuống Quầy Pha Chế!").ConfigureAwait(false);
            return count;
        }

        public async Task RemoveBillDetailItemAsync(int billDetailId, int tableId, string tableName, string itemName, bool isSentToKitchen, string managerName)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.REMOVE_ITEM_REQ, new { TableId = tableId, BillDetailId = billDetailId }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
            if (isSentToKitchen)
                await SendChatAsync($"⚠️ [CẢNH BÁO HỦY MÓN] Bàn: {tableName} | Món: {itemName} | Duyệt: {managerName}").ConfigureAwait(false);
        }

        public async Task MoveToEmptyTableAsync(int sourceTableId, int targetTableId)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.MOVE_TABLE_REQ, new { SourceTableId = sourceTableId, TargetTableId = targetTableId }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
        }

        public async Task MergeOccupiedTablesAsync(int sourceTableId, int targetTableId)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.MERGE_TABLE_REQ, new { SourceTableId = sourceTableId, TargetTableId = targetTableId }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
        }

        public async Task<CheckoutResponseDTO> CheckoutTableAsync(int tableId, string tableName, int discountPercent, string paymentMethod)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.CHECKOUT_REQ, new
            {
                TableId = tableId,
                DiscountPercent = discountPercent,
                PaymentMethod = paymentMethod
            }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            var dto = DeserializeOrThrow<CheckoutResponseDTO>(res);
            if (dto.IsSuccess)
                await SendChatAsync($"💰 [THANH TOÁN] {tableName}: +{dto.FinalAmount:N0} VNĐ ({paymentMethod})").ConfigureAwait(false);
            return dto;
        }

        public async Task<Bitmap> GetVietQrBitmapAsync(decimal amount, string tableName)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.GET_VIETQR_REQ, new { Amount = amount, TableName = tableName }, 15000).ConfigureAwait(false);
            var dto = DeserializeOrThrow<QrResponseDTO>(res);
            byte[] bytes = Convert.FromBase64String(dto.Base64Png);
            using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(bytes))
            using (Image img = Image.FromStream(ms))
                return new Bitmap(img);
        }

        public DataTable GetPendingKitchenItems()
        {
            var res = SendRequestAsync(MsgType.GET_PENDING_KDS_REQ).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            var items = DeserializeOrThrow<List<KitchenItemDTO>>(res);

            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            dt.Columns.Add("Mã", typeof(int));
            dt.Columns.Add("Bàn / Đơn", typeof(string));
            dt.Columns.Add("Tên món cần pha", typeof(string));
            dt.Columns.Add("Số lượng", typeof(int));
            dt.Columns.Add("Giờ gọi", typeof(string));
            dt.Columns.Add("Trạng thái", typeof(string));

            foreach (var it in items)
                dt.Rows.Add(it.BillDetailId, it.TableName, it.ProductName, it.Quantity, it.OrderTime, it.KitchenStatus);
            return dt;
        }

        public async Task MarkKitchenItemDoneAsync(int billDetailId, string tableName, string productName)
        {
            var res = await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.KDS_COMPLETE_ITEM_REQ, new { BillDetailId = billDetailId }).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (!res.Success) throw new Exception(res.Message);
            await SendChatAsync($"✅ [Quầy Pha Chế]: Đã làm xong [{productName}] cho [{tableName}]!").ConfigureAwait(false);
        }

        public async Task SendChatAsync(string text)
        {
            try { await SendRequestAsync(MsgType.CHAT_MESSAGE, new { Text = text }).ConfigureAwait(false); }
            catch { }
        }

        public void ShowInAppChatAndStatsDialog(Form parentForm, string currentRoleName = "Nhân viên")
        {
            if (_activeChatForm != null && !_activeChatForm.IsDisposed)
            {
                _activeChatForm.BringToFront();
                return;
            }

            Form f = new Form { Text = $"Chat nội bộ ({ServerHost}:{ServerPort})", Size = new Size(560, 440), StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent };
            _activeChatForm = f;

            RichTextBox rtb = new RichTextBox { Location = new Point(12, 12), Size = new Size(520, 330), ReadOnly = true };
            TextBox txt = new TextBox { Location = new Point(12, 355), Size = new Size(410, 30) };
            Button btn = new Button { Text = "Gửi", Location = new Point(430, 353), Size = new Size(102, 32) };

            Action refresh = () =>
            {
                lock (ChatHistory) rtb.Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, ChatHistory);
                rtb.SelectionStart = rtb.Text.Length;
                rtb.ScrollToCaret();
            };

            Action<MsgType, string> onEvt = (t, p) =>
            {
                if (t == MsgType.CHAT_MESSAGE && !f.IsDisposed && f.IsHandleCreated)
                    f.BeginInvoke(new Action(refresh));
            };

            OnServerEventReceived += onEvt;
            f.FormClosing += (s, e) => OnServerEventReceived -= onEvt;
            btn.Click += async (s, e) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txt.Text)) return;
                string msg = $"[{currentRoleName} - {DateTime.Now:HH:mm}]: {txt.Text.Trim()}";
                txt.Clear();
                await SendChatAsync(msg);
            };

            f.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { rtb, txt, btn });
            refresh();
            f.Show(parentForm);
        }
    }
}


/* =========================================
   FILE: SmartPaymentService.cs 
========================================= */

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Drawing.Imaging;
using System.IO;
using System.Media;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.Services
{
    public class ApiRequest
    {
        public string accountNo { get; set; }
        public string accountName { get; set; }
        public int acqId { get; set; }
        public long amount { get; set; }
        public string addInfo { get; set; }
        public string format { get; set; }
        public string template { get; set; }
    }

    public static class SmartPaymentService
    {
        // Thông tin tài khoản Vietcombank chính chủ của Nguyễn Hoàng Duy Anh
        public static string BankId = "970436";
        public static int AcqId = 970436;
        public static string BankShortName = "Vietcombank";
        public static string AccountNo = "9942886521";
        public static string AccountName = "NGUYEN HOANG DUY ANH";
        public static string Template = "compact2";

        // Ưu tiên gọi VietQR API v2 (POST JSON -> Base64), lỗi mạng tự chuyển sang vẽ thẻ QR dự phòng
        public static Bitmap GetQrBitmap(decimal amount, string tableName)
        {
            long roundedAmount = Convert.ToInt64(Math.Round(amount));
            string cleanTable = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName) ? "Ban Cafe" : tableName.Trim();
            string cleanInfo = RemoveVietnameseAccents($"Thanh toan {cleanTable}").Trim();
            if (cleanInfo.Length > 25) cleanInfo = cleanInfo.Substring(0, 25);

            try
            {
                string jsonRequest = "{"
                    + $"\"accountNo\":\"{AccountNo}\","
                    + $"\"accountName\":\"{AccountName}\","
                    + $"\"acqId\":{AcqId},"
                    + $"\"amount\":{roundedAmount},"
                    + $"\"addInfo\":\"{cleanInfo}\","
                    + $"\"format\":\"text\","
                    + $"\"template\":\"{Template}\""
                    + "}";

                ServicePointManager.Expect100Continue = true;
                ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls12 | (SecurityProtocolType)3072;

                using (WebClient client = new WebClient())
                {
                    client.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
                    client.Headers[HttpRequestHeader.ContentType] = "application/json";
                    client.Headers[HttpRequestHeader.Accept] = "application/json";
                    client.Headers.Add("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)");

                    string responseJson = client.UploadString("https://api.vietqr.io/v2/generate", "POST", jsonRequest);
                    Match match = Regex.Match(responseJson, @"""qrDataURL""\s*:\s*""([^""]+)""");
                    if (match.Success)
                    {
                        string qrDataURL = match.Groups[1].Value.Replace(@"\/", "/");
                        string base64String = qrDataURL.Replace("data:image/png;base64,", "").Trim();
                        return Base64ToImage(base64String);
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Chuyển sang cơ chế dự phòng bên dưới nếu API v2 lỗi hoặc mất Internet
            }

            try
            {
                string emvPayload = BuildNapas247Payload(roundedAmount, cleanInfo);
                string backupQrUrl = $"https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=360x360&margin=10&data={Uri.EscapeDataString(emvPayload)}";

                using (WebClient wc = new WebClient())
                {
                    wc.Headers.Add("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)");
                    byte[] qrBytes = wc.DownloadData(backupQrUrl);
                    if (qrBytes != null && qrBytes.Length >= 200)
                    {
                        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(qrBytes))
                        using (Image qrImg = Image.FromStream(ms))
                        {
                            return DrawVietcombankCard(qrImg, roundedAmount);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            Image localQr = TryLoadLocalFallbackQr();
            Bitmap offlineBmp = DrawVietcombankCard(localQr, roundedAmount);
            if (localQr != null) localQr.Dispose();
            return offlineBmp;
        }

        public static Bitmap Base64ToImage(string base64String)
        {
            byte[] imageBytes = Convert.FromBase64String(base64String);
            using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(imageBytes))
            using (Image img = Image.FromStream(ms, true))
            {
                return new Bitmap(img);
            }
        }

        public static string BuildVietQrUrl(decimal amount, string tableName)
        {
            string tempFilePath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"vietqr_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.png");
            using (Bitmap bmp = GetQrBitmap(amount, tableName))
            {
                bmp.Save(tempFilePath, ImageFormat.Png);
            }
            return tempFilePath;
        }

        private static Image TryLoadLocalFallbackQr()
        {
            string baseDir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
            string[] candidateNames = { "vietqr.png", "vietqr.jpg", "1794504598098.png", "qr_vcb.png" };
            foreach (string name in candidateNames)
            {
                string fullPath = Path.Combine(baseDir, name);
                if (File.Exists(fullPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        byte[] bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(fullPath);
                        return Image.FromStream(new MemoryStream(bytes));
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        private static string BuildNapas247Payload(long amount, string description)
        {
            string accField = $"0006{BankId}01{AccountNo.Length:D2}{AccountNo}";
            string serviceField = $"0010A00000072701{accField.Length:D2}{accField}0208QRIBFTTA";
            string merchantTag38 = $"38{serviceField.Length:D2}{serviceField}";

            string amountStr = amount.ToString();
            string amountTag54 = amount > 0 ? $"54{amountStr.Length:D2}{amountStr}" : "";

            string cleanDesc = RemoveVietnameseAccents(description);
            if (cleanDesc.Length > 25) cleanDesc = cleanDesc.Substring(0, 25);
            string subTag08 = $"08{cleanDesc.Length:D2}{cleanDesc}";
            string addDataTag62 = $"62{subTag08.Length:D2}{subTag08}";

            string rawPayload = $"000201010212{merchantTag38}5303704{amountTag54}5802VN{addDataTag62}6304";
            string crc = ComputeCrc16Ccitt(rawPayload);
            return rawPayload + crc;
        }

        private static string ComputeCrc16Ccitt(string input)
        {
            ushort crc = 0xFFFF;
            byte[] bytes = Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(input);
            foreach (byte b in bytes)
            {
                crc ^= (ushort)(b << 8);
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
                {
                    if ((crc & 0x8000) != 0)
                        crc = (ushort)((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021);
                    else
                        crc <<= 1;
                }
            }
            return crc.ToString("X4");
        }

        private static string RemoveVietnameseAccents(string text)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) return "Thanh toan";
            string normalized = text.Normalize(NormalizationForm.FormD);
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            foreach (char c in normalized)
            {
                System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory uc = System.Globalization.CharUnicodeInfo.GetUnicodeCategory(c);
                if (uc != System.Globalization.UnicodeCategory.NonSpacingMark)
                {
                    sb.Append(c);
                }
            }
            return sb.ToString().Normalize(NormalizationForm.FormC).Replace('đ', 'd').Replace('Đ', 'D');
        }

        private static Bitmap DrawVietcombankCard(Image qrImage, long amount)
        {
            Bitmap bmp = new Bitmap(360, 460);
            using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(bmp))
            {
                g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
                g.Clear(Color.FromArgb(242, 249, 235));

                using (SolidBrush cardBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.White))
                {
                    g.FillRectangle(cardBrush, 20, 20, 320, 420);
                }

                StringFormat sf = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Center };
                using (Font fBank = new Font("Segoe UI", 15, FontStyle.Bold))
                using (SolidBrush brGreen = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(0, 107, 63)))
                {
                    g.DrawString("Vietcombank", fBank, brGreen, new RectangleF(0, 28, 360, 32), sf);
                }

                if (qrImage != null)
                {
                    g.DrawImage(qrImage, new Rectangle(55, 68, 250, 250));
                }
                else
                {
                    DrawOfflineQrMatrix(g, new Rectangle(65, 78, 230, 230));
                }

                using (Font fSub = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f, FontStyle.Bold))
                using (SolidBrush brRed = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(192, 57, 43)))
                {
                    g.DrawString("VIETQR - NAPAS 247", fSub, brRed, new RectangleF(0, 328, 360, 22), sf);
                }

                using (Font fName = new Font("Segoe UI", 11, FontStyle.Bold))
                using (Font fAcc = new Font("Segoe UI", 11, FontStyle.Regular))
                using (SolidBrush brDark = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 30, 30)))
                {
                    g.DrawString(AccountName, fName, brDark, new RectangleF(0, 358, 360, 24), sf);
                    g.DrawString($"STK: {AccountNo}", fAcc, brDark, new RectangleF(0, 382, 360, 24), sf);
                    if (amount > 0)
                    {
                        g.DrawString($"Số tiền: {amount:N0} VNĐ", fName, brDark, new RectangleF(0, 408, 360, 24), sf);
                    }
                }
            }
            return bmp;
        }

        private static void DrawOfflineQrMatrix(Graphics g, Rectangle bounds)
        {
            int gridSize = 21;
            int cellSize = bounds.Width / gridSize;
            Random rnd = new Random(994288652);

            using (SolidBrush black = new SolidBrush(Color.Black))
            using (Pen borderPen = new Pen(Color.FromArgb(0, 107, 63), 2))
            {
                g.DrawRectangle(borderPen, bounds.X - 6, bounds.Y - 6, bounds.Width + 12, bounds.Height + 12);

                for (int r = 0; r < gridSize; r++)
                {
                    for (int c = 0; c < gridSize; c++)
                    {
                        bool isFinder = (r < 7 && c < 7) || (r < 7 && c >= gridSize - 7) || (r >= gridSize - 7 && c < 7);
                        if (isFinder)
                        {
                            int lr = r >= gridSize - 7 ? r - (gridSize - 7) : r;
                            int lc = c >= gridSize - 7 ? c - (gridSize - 7) : c;
                            if (lr == 0 || lr == 6 || lc == 0 || lc == 6 || (lr >= 2 && lr <= 4 && lc >= 2 && lc <= 4))
                            {
                                g.FillRectangle(black, bounds.X + c * cellSize, bounds.Y + r * cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);
                            }
                        }
                        else if (rnd.Next(100) < 48)
                        {
                            g.FillRectangle(black, bounds.X + c * cellSize, bounds.Y + r * cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public static void PlaySuccessSound()
        {
            SystemSounds.Asterisk.Play();
        }
    }
}


/* =========================================
   FILE: TcpOrderService.cs 
========================================= */

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.Services
{
    public class TcpOrderService
    {
        // ================= PHẦN TCP SERVER (DÀNH CHO FrmMainPOS) =================
        private TcpListener _server;
        private readonly List<TcpClient> _connectedClients = new List<TcpClient>();
        private bool _isServerRunning = false;

        public event Action<string> OnServerReceivedMessage;
        public event Action<int> OnClientCountChanged;

        public void StartServer(int port = 8888)
        {
            if (_isServerRunning) return;
            _isServerRunning = true;

            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                try
                {
                    _server = new TcpListener(IPAddress.Any, port);
                    _server.Start();

                    while (_isServerRunning)
                    {
                        TcpClient client = await _server.AcceptTcpClientAsync();
                        lock (_connectedClients)
                        {
                            _connectedClients.Add(client);
                        }
                        OnClientCountChanged?.Invoke(_connectedClients.Count);

                        // Lắng nghe phản hồi từ Client (KDS báo hoàn thành món)
                        _ = ListenToClientAsync(client);
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            });
        }

        private async Task ListenToClientAsync(TcpClient client)
        {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
            try
            {
                NetworkStream stream = client.GetStream();
                while (_isServerRunning && client.Connected)
                {
                    int bytesRead = await stream.ReadAsync(buffer, 0, buffer.Length);
                    if (bytesRead == 0) break;

                    string msg = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
                    OnServerReceivedMessage?.Invoke(msg);
                }
            }
            catch { }
            finally
            {
                lock (_connectedClients)
                {
                    _connectedClients.Remove(client);
                }
                OnClientCountChanged?.Invoke(_connectedClients.Count);
            }
        }

        // Server bắn gói tin sang tất cả màn hình KDS đang kết nối
        public void BroadcastToClients(string message)
        {
            byte[] data = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(message);
            lock (_connectedClients)
            {
                for (int i = _connectedClients.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (_connectedClients[i].Connected)
                        {
                            NetworkStream stream = _connectedClients[i].GetStream();
                            stream.Write(data, 0, data.Length);
                        }
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        _connectedClients.RemoveAt(i);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public void StopServer()
        {
            _isServerRunning = false;
            try { _server?.Stop(); } catch { }
        }

        // ================= PHẦN TCP CLIENT (DÀNH CHO FrmKDS) =================
        private TcpClient _client;
        private NetworkStream _clientStream;

        public event Action<string> OnClientReceivedMessage;
        public event Action<bool> OnConnectionStatusChanged;

        public bool IsClientConnected => _client != null && _client.Connected;

        public async Task<bool> ConnectToServerAsync(string ip = "127.0.0.1", int port = 8888)
        {
            try
            {
                if (IsClientConnected) return true;

                _client = new TcpClient();
                await _client.ConnectAsync(ip, port);
                _clientStream = _client.GetStream();
                OnConnectionStatusChanged?.Invoke(true);

                // Bắt đầu lắng nghe gói tin từ Server (Thu ngân gửi món mới)
                _ = Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
                    try
                    {
                        while (_client != null && _client.Connected)
                        {
                            int bytesRead = await _clientStream.ReadAsync(buffer, 0, buffer.Length);
                            if (bytesRead == 0) break;

                            string msg = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
                            OnClientReceivedMessage?.Invoke(msg);
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    finally
                    {
                        OnConnectionStatusChanged?.Invoke(false);
                    }
                });

                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                OnConnectionStatusChanged?.Invoke(false);
                return false;
            }
        }

        // Client (KDS) bắn gói tin ngược về Server (Thu ngân)
        public void SendToServer(string message)
        {
            try
            {
                if (IsClientConnected && _clientStream != null)
                {
                    byte[] data = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(message);
                    _clientStream.Write(data, 0, data.Length);
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }
}


/* =========================================
   FILE: TelegramBotService.cs 
========================================= */

using System;
using System.Data;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.DAL;

namespace QuanLyCafe_Nhom8.Services
{
    public class TelegramBotService
    {
        // ThÃ´ng tin cáº¥u hÃ¬nh Bot Token vÃ  Chat ID cá»§a nhÃ³m
        private readonly string botToken = "9079144131:AAH0X7MrQPP1xsoRV1KbW2L8Hmk7q0Eg7x4";
        private readonly string chatId = "5939667192";

        // Biáº¿n tÄ©nh Ä‘áº£m báº£o luá»“ng láº¯ng nghe tin nháº¯n 2 chiá»u chá»‰ khá»Ÿi cháº¡y 1 láº§n duy nháº¥t
        private static bool _isPollingStarted = false;
        private static long _lastUpdateId = 0;

        public TelegramBotService()
        {
            StartTwoWayListener();
        }

        // Khá»Ÿi cháº¡y luá»“ng cháº¡y ngáº§m (Background Task) láº¯ng nghe lá»‡nh tá»« Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i cá»§a Quáº£n lÃ½ má»—i 3 giÃ¢y
        public void StartTwoWayListener()
        {
            if (_isPollingStarted) return;
            _isPollingStarted = true;

            Task.Run(() =>
            {
                ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls12;
                bool isFirstSync = true;

                while (true)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        string url = $"https://api.telegram.org/bot{botToken}/getUpdates?offset={_lastUpdateId + 1}&timeout=0";
                        string json = "";

                        using (WebClient wc = new WebClient())
                        {
                            wc.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
                            json = wc.DownloadString(url);
                        }

                        // TÃ¡ch tá»«ng tin nháº¯n má»›i gá»­i tá»›i Bot báº±ng biá»ƒu thá»©c chÃ­nh quy (Regex)
                        MatchCollection matches = Regex.Matches(
                            json,
                            @"""update_id""\s*:\s*(\d+).*?""text""\s*:\s*""([^""]+)""",
                            RegexOptions.Singleline);

                        foreach (Match m in matches)
                        {
                            long updateId = Convert.ToInt64(m.Groups[1].Value);
                            string rawText = m.Groups[2].Value.Replace(@"\/", "/").Trim();

                            if (updateId > _lastUpdateId)
                            {
                                _lastUpdateId = updateId;

                                // Bá» qua cÃ¡c tin nháº¯n cÅ© tá»“n Ä‘á»ng trÆ°á»›c lÃºc má»Ÿ pháº§n má»m
                                if (!isFirstSync)
                                {
                                    ProcessManagerCommand(rawText);
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        isFirstSync = false;
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        // Bá» qua lá»—i máº¥t máº¡ng táº¡m thá»i Ä‘á»ƒ vÃ²ng láº·p tiáº¿p tá»¥c cháº¡y á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh
                    }

                    Thread.Sleep(3000);
                }
            });
        }

        // Xá»­ lÃ½ lá»‡nh khi Quáº£n lÃ½ gÃµ /homnay, /homqua, /doanhthu, /ban, /huymon trÃªn Telegram
        private void ProcessManagerCommand(string commandText)
        {
            string cmd = commandText.ToLowerInvariant().Split(' ')[0];

            if (cmd == "/homnay" || cmd == "/doanhthu")
            {
                GuiTin(LayBaoCaoDoanhThuTheoNgay(DateTime.Today, "HÃ”M NAY"));
            }
            else if (cmd == "/homqua")
            {
                GuiTin(LayBaoCaoDoanhThuTheoNgay(DateTime.Today.AddDays(-1), "HÃ”M QUA"));
            }
            else if (cmd == "/ban")
            {
                GuiTin(LayTrangThaiBanHienTai());
            }
            else if (cmd == "/huymon")
            {
                BaoHuyMon();
            }
            else if (cmd == "/help" || cmd == "/start")
            {
                string helpMsg = "ðŸ¤– MENU TRá»¢ LÃ QUáº¢N LÃ CAFE NHÃ“M 8:\n"
                               + "ðŸ‘‰ /homnay : Xem doanh thu & sá»‘ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ´m nay\n"
                               + "ðŸ‘‰ /homqua : Xem doanh thu & sá»‘ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ´m qua\n"
                               + "ðŸ‘‰ /ban : Xem danh sÃ¡ch bÃ n Ä‘ang cÃ³ khÃ¡ch\n"
                               + "ðŸ‘‰ /huymon : Kiá»ƒm tra lá»‹ch sá»­ há»§y mÃ³n hÃ´m nay";
                GuiTin(helpMsg);
            }
        }

        // Truy váº¥n doanh thu theo ngÃ y (HÃ´m nay hoáº·c HÃ´m qua) tá»« báº£ng Bills
        private string LayBaoCaoDoanhThuTheoNgay(DateTime targetDate, string label)
        {
            try
            {
                string sql = @"
                    SELECT COUNT(*) AS SoDon, 
                           ISNULL(SUM(TotalAmount), 0) AS TongTien
                    FROM Bills
                    WHERE Status = 1 AND CAST(CheckOutTime AS DATE) = @Ngay";

                DataTable dt = DatabaseHelper.ExecuteQuery(sql, new System.Data.SqlClient.SqlParameter[]
                {
                    new System.Data.SqlClient.SqlParameter("@Ngay", targetDate.Date)
                });

                int soDon = Convert.ToInt32(dt.Rows[0]["SoDon"]);
                decimal tongTien = Convert.ToDecimal(dt.Rows[0]["TongTien"]);

                string detailSql = @"
                    SELECT ISNULL(PaymentMethod, N'Tiá»n máº·t') AS PhuongThuc, 
                           COUNT(*) AS SL, 
                           ISNULL(SUM(TotalAmount), 0) AS Tien
                    FROM Bills
                    WHERE Status = 1 AND CAST(CheckOutTime AS DATE) = @Ngay
                    GROUP BY ISNULL(PaymentMethod, N'Tiá»n máº·t')";

                DataTable dtDetail = DatabaseHelper.ExecuteQuery(detailSql, new System.Data.SqlClient.SqlParameter[]
                {
                    new System.Data.SqlClient.SqlParameter("@Ngay", targetDate.Date)
                });

                StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                sb.AppendLine($"ðŸ“Š BÃO CÃO DOANH THU {label} ({targetDate:dd/MM/yyyy})");
                sb.AppendLine($"- Tá»•ng sá»‘ Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Ã£ thu: {soDon} Ä‘Æ¡n");
                sb.AppendLine($"- Tá»•ng doanh thu: {tongTien:N0} VNÄ");

                if (dtDetail.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    sb.AppendLine("--------------------------");
                    sb.AppendLine("Chi tiáº¿t theo nguá»“n tiá»n:");
                    foreach (DataRow r in dtDetail.Rows)
                    {
                        sb.AppendLine($"â€¢ {r["PhuongThuc"]}: {r["SL"]} Ä‘Æ¡n ({Convert.ToDecimal(r["Tien"]):N0} VNÄ)");
                    }
                }

                return sb.ToString();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return "Lá»—i tra cá»©u doanh thu: " + ex.Message;
            }
        }

        // Truy váº¥n cÃ¡c bÃ n Ä‘ang cÃ³ khÃ¡ch phá»¥c vá»¥ khi Quáº£n lÃ½ gÃµ /ban
        private string LayTrangThaiBanHienTai()
        {
            try
            {
                string sql = @"
                    SELECT t.TableName, b.TotalAmount
                    FROM Tables t
                    INNER JOIN Bills b ON t.TableId = b.TableId
                    WHERE b.Status = 0";
                DataTable dt = DatabaseHelper.ExecuteQuery(sql);

                if (dt.Rows.Count == 0)
                {
                    return "ðŸŸ¢ Hiá»‡n táº¡i táº¥t cáº£ cÃ¡c bÃ n Ä‘á»u Ä‘ang TRá»NG.";
                }

                StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                sb.AppendLine($"ðŸŸ  ÄANG PHá»¤C Vá»¤: {dt.Rows.Count} BÃ€N / ÄÆ N:");
                foreach (DataRow r in dt.Rows)
                {
                    sb.AppendLine($"â€¢ {r["TableName"]}: táº¡m tÃ­nh {Convert.ToDecimal(r["TotalAmount"]):N0} VNÄ");
                }
                return sb.ToString();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return "Lá»—i kiá»ƒm tra bÃ n: " + ex.Message;
            }
        }

        // HÃ m gá»­i tin nháº¯n 1 chiá»u lÃªn Telegram
        public bool GuiTin(string text)
        {
            try
            {
                ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls12;
                string url = $"https://api.telegram.org/bot{botToken}/sendMessage?chat_id={chatId}&text={Uri.EscapeDataString(text)}";

                using (WebClient wc = new WebClient())
                {
                    wc.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
                    wc.DownloadString(url);
                }
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        // HÃ m gá»­i bÃ¡o cÃ¡o doanh thu tá»« mÃ n hÃ¬nh FrmStatistics
        public bool GuiDoanhThu(DateTime tu, DateTime den, int soDon, decimal tong)
        {
            string msg = $"ðŸ“Š BÃO CÃO DOANH THU ({tu:dd/MM/yyyy} - {den:dd/MM/yyyy})\n"
                       + $"- Sá»‘ hÃ³a Ä‘Æ¡n: {soDon}\n"
                       + $"- Tá»•ng tiá»n: {tong:N0} VNÄ";
            return GuiTin(msg);
        }

        // HÃ m kiá»ƒm tra vÃ  gá»­i cáº£nh bÃ¡o cÃ¡c mÃ³n bá»‹ há»§y trong ngÃ y tá»« báº£ng AuditLogs
        public string BaoHuyMon()
        {
            try
            {
                string sql = @"
                    SELECT TOP 5 a.LogId, a.BillId, p.ProductName, a.DeletedQuantity, FORMAT(a.LogTime, 'HH:mm:ss') AS GioHuy
                    FROM AuditLogs a
                    LEFT JOIN Products p ON a.ProductId = p.ProductId
                    WHERE CAST(a.LogTime AS DATE) = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                    ORDER BY a.LogId DESC";
                DataTable dt = DatabaseHelper.ExecuteQuery(sql);

                if (dt.Rows.Count == 0)
                {
                    GuiTin("âœ… HÃ´m nay quÃ¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ thao tÃ¡c há»§y mÃ³n nÃ o.");
                    return "HÃ´m nay khÃ´ng cÃ³ há»§y mÃ³n.";
                }

                string msg = "âš ï¸ Cáº¢NH BÃO Há»¦Y MÃ“N HÃ”M NAY:\n";
                foreach (DataRow row in dt.Rows)
                {
                    msg += $"- ÄÆ¡n #{row["BillId"]} | MÃ³n: {row["ProductName"]} | SL há»§y: {row["DeletedQuantity"]} | LÃºc: {row["GioHuy"]}\n";
                }

                return GuiTin(msg) ? "ÄÃ£ gá»­i Telegram!" : "Gá»­i tháº¥t báº¡i!";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return "Lá»—i: " + ex.Message;
            }
        }
    }
